Initial findings: narrowing unison spacing from 0.8 to 0.2 cents reduces held
G4's detrended 3.5–6.5 s envelope excursion from 11.71 to 2.15 dB. Removing
only dynamic duplexes changes nothing; removing the entire silent bank still
leaves 8.25 dB. The duplex-only hypothesis is therefore insufficient. The
estimated C3 duplex fundamental also accounts for the resolved 396.6 Hz line
beside G4. A wider/stronger pad (0.08 m / 0.8 N s/m) reduces C2's 4–6 s tail
by 6.83 dB; stronger-contact and low-register holdouts remain in progress.
None of these measurements establishes naturalness.

bit-identical held bass followed by dissipative release; and energy accounting
through sustain and restrike with added duplex loss. The additional controls
default to the original values. A local `withVoicing` browser constructor keeps
the published loss law while changing only the named physical inputs. No live
tone default has changed. The first held-note A/B is available to the owner;
musical comparisons, browser qualification and listening disposition are pending.

### Rejection audit (owner-requested, 2026-10-27)

**Three additional FrankenSim levers were held back solely by the same arbitrary
5% cutoff.** They are active coefficient packing, loss-share caching and fixed
force/energy-product caching. Together with browser bank pairing, this makes
four affected levers. This was a decision error, not an acoustic failure.

| Candidate | Recorded evidence | Current disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Active packing | Bit-exact; full-app native 3.59%, browser constitutive profile executed natively 2.49% (confirmation 2.38%). With FFT: 14.96% / 9.35% versus direct receiver. | Enabled in the measured native and SIMD-browser stacks. |
| Loss share cache | Bit-exact samples and ledgers; 2.13% median paired gain. | Enabled in the measured SIMD-browser stack; retained selectable in native. |
| Fixed force/energy products | Bit-exact; 0.40% alone; both caches 3.31%. | Enabled in the measured native and SIMD-browser stacks. |
| Browser bank pairing | Bit-exact; final enabled build 3.52%, earlier trials 6.23% / 8.01%. Native 0.061%. | Browser enabled and verified with packing/caches; native pairing still opt-in. |
| FrankenPiano PGO | Three corrected controlled runs improved mean 0.16–0.60%; one REAPER pair mean 0.62%, p99 1.12%, no observed xruns, exact controlled PCM. Opposite-order host confirmation never ran. | Reopen consideration: not a demonstrated loss. Qualification/maintenance remains to measure, not a reason to describe it as worthless. |
| FrankenPiano four-RHS triangular solve | Exact eigenvalues/vectors; 1.12–1.18x at rank 128 in an x86 microprobe. | Unfinished product verification, not a failed optimization; no whole-piano gain yet. |

The broader read covered `research/perf/{worst-pedal-blocks,apple-live-neon,
neon-tail-bounds,woodbury-packed}-2026-10-26.md` and `{active-coefficients,
mode-candidates,string-pair-and-pgo,reverse-traversal-and-sme,cold-rebuild}-2026-10-27.md`
in `/Users/jemanuel/projects/frankenpiano_vst`. This is a bounded audit of the
recorded experiments, not a claim to have recovered every historical attempt.
FrankenPiano already retained roughly 1% tail-bounds reuse and sub-1% Woodbury
packing, so small gains were not consistently rejected across the project.

Other dispositions had more substantive evidence: independent-string pairing
lost all three engine pairs; compact post and extra unsafe slice reuse lost in
REAPER; SME was several times slower; the new QL solver improved some rebuild
times but raised callback mean in both DAW pairs. Reverse traversal's mean gain
coincided with unresolved underrun observations and a worse maximum. FMA,
contact specialization, segment-map reuse, four-lane reductions and inline/
unrolled passes had mixed results; preserve them as inconclusive or workload-
specific rather than calling every one universally slower. This pass does not
silently enable changed rounding, unverified solvers or observed regressions.

Stack measurement uses the existing full-piano test seam, all 16 combinations
of the four independent flags, the same events and full ledgers, six alternating
rounds, and a preserved pre-cache browser binary in the same run. Percentages
are measured together, not added. No arbitrary minimum CPU percentage remains;
retain reproducible useful gains while checking sound and tail behavior.

remain bit-identical; FFT/combined output must satisfy the existing -100 dB
relative power bound and unchanged energy accounting. Each enabled lever must
improve whole-piano median paired wall time by at least 5%, with no repeatable
block-tail regression. Report all arms and rounds; retain the direct path and
leave any inconclusive or losing lever opt-in.

## Earlier receiver-only checkpoint

These historical attempts explain the original blockers. The follow-through
and whole-piano results above supersede their pending-kernel status.

No source changes or timings from another repository establish upstream success.
Mac disk preflight refuses local heavy builds despite 267 GB available; use the
remote fleet, without changing disk thresholds or touching other agents' caches.

Build admission: ordinary RCH refused unfinished overlapping source owners on
three workers; ts1 and vmi1264463 queued requests timed out. Exact-source receipt
mode refuses nested asupersync roots. Clean-overlay mode refuses the SQLite
submodule, or refuses the missing selected SQLite dependency if left unbound.
No source locks, peer files, or build policies were changed to bypass these.

Ordinary RCH on `ovh-b` subsequently admitted the job and began compilation:
`RCH_REQUIRE_REMOTE=1 rch exec --worker ovh-b -- cargo test --manifest-path
apps/instrument-lab/engine-piano/Cargo.toml --test receiver_backport --release -j 2`.
Build `30083054965882959`, wrapper
`rchw-e1fc9ba7-4c39-4951-b8df-5ee9dc2459d8`. It reached the compiler and failed in
`fs-geom` (`router.rs`: missing `receipt`; `convert.rs`: `Certified<SampledSdf>`
where `Certified<f64>` is required). Both repairs compiled on the next attempt,
which failed on `PropertyValue::Surface2D` in the temperature-only material
loader. Explicit unsupported-input handling compiled on the following attempt.
That attempt failed in fs-lbm: missing fs-mesh::overlap and
FreeSurfaceInitialCell3/FreeSurface3::from_cells, plus cascading type errors.
Those modules have existing staged removals and other agents' replacement work;
they have not been restored or rewritten for this task.
The existing live kernel lacks material methods still called by audio/lib;
await compiler evidence before attributing a build failure to either backport.

Focused verification target: `apps/instrument-lab/engine-piano/receiver-check`.
It compiles the production receiver files directly and shares the integration
tests with the full piano target. Its optional `piano` feature imports the actual
kernel and cold preparation modules, excluding application host wrappers and
the unused offline Instrument oracle. Its 45-crate source closure excludes
fs-lbm. This is numerical/native-kernel evidence only, not browser/DAW evidence.
The first focused RCH build found the receiver's wind-network adapter dependency.
A check-target-only cfg excludes that adapter, retaining it in every normal
fs-couple feature configuration. The second focused build reached fs-plate and
found its missing shell pressure-module declaration; that repair compiled on
retry. The next failure is fs-vfit's declared `quadratic_loss` module whose source
is staged deleted and absent. No consumer remains in the current tree, but its
module declaration is an unstaged peer addition; neither has been changed.
WindyKite was notified (message 8446). Native active-set/combined verification and
the complete application build remain pending reconciliation of that work.
All compiler logs are under the external
`frankensim-backport-20261027` evidence directory.

Final native receiver run on ovh-b used `cargo test --manifest-path
apps/instrument-lab/engine-piano/receiver-check/Cargo.toml --lib --release -j 2
-- --include-ignored --nocapture --test-threads=1` through RCH:
42 passed, 1 failed, 0 ignored in 4.31 s. All new backport checks passed. The
failure is `rectangular_flow_directivity_converges_to_the_independent_far_field_sinc_product`:
errors `[0.01187841454772709, 0.002961019807897536, 0.0007487778142536428]`,
last exceeding its unchanged `5e-4` assertion. That test only inspects the
original geometric FIR kernels and never enables FFT; its constructor/math
were not changed by this backport. It remains a failure, not a passing suite.
Paired receiver timings include the incumbent in the SAME process. First,
the general fs-fft plan lost: 32-mode median 31.643 -> 37.892 ms and 128-mode
median 114.689 -> 136.675 ms (`receiver-performance.log`). That implementation
was replaced with the bounded donor plan, using fs-math deterministic twiddles.
The final paired run (`small-fft-comparison.log`) measured 2.77–2.97x at 32 modes
and 2.97–3.28x at 128 modes, with all measured output residuals below -284 dB.
Each arm processes 12,800 frames at 48 kHz, 24 surface patches, half driven and
half silence, in 100 blocks of 128 frames. Three pairs reverse order on the
middle run. Construction is excluded; p99/max block times are in the log.
These are receiver-only CPU and numerical-equivalence results on x86 ovh-b,
not whole-piano/DAW/browser performance or listener acceptance.

repository toolchain pin. The retry passed (exit 0): `cargo check --manifest-path
apps/instrument-lab/engine-piano/receiver-check/Cargo.toml --lib --target
wasm32-unknown-unknown -j 2` via RCH on ovh-b, build 30083054965883014.
Existing dependency warnings remain. This compiles the production receiver and
bounded FFT for WASM; it neither runs WASM audio nor compiles the active kernel.
Log: `receiver-wasm-check-retry.log`. The focused target's resolved Cargo.lock
was copied from this worker run; the prior lock is preserved with the evidence.

Final preservation check: all 11 recorded Git index entries match their starting
values. The existing piano benchmark and fs-couple/Cargo.toml are byte-identical
to their starting contents. Only focused working-tree changes remain; nothing
was staged, committed, pushed, deployed or charged. All new Rust files pass

The active Beads dependency graph has no cycles.

The still-unrun piano benchmark runs the original, FFT-only, packing-only and combined
arms in one process, reversing order on the middle round. It checks output
residuals before reporting all three rounds' wall, p99 and maximum block times.
No active-set or whole-piano speedup/sound-preservation result is claimed before

Tracking artifact gate: explicitly requested by Jeffrey; consumer is Jeffrey
and the implementing agent; prevents losing the two accepted tasks and their
unrun checks; retires into the Bead on completion. This is bookkeeping, not a
performance result. Baseline snapshots protect pre-existing dirty edits and
